Result: 给出了多个单模示例验证该方法的有效性，提出了基于被动干涉测量和光子数分辨探测的实验协议，表明相关多拷贝期望值可以从有界相位观测量估计得到。还展示了该框架可扩展到多模系统，并为定量非高斯性度量提供潜在途径。

Conclusion: 该工作为连续变量量子信息中的非高斯性检测提供了一种新的群论框架，不仅补充了现有的Wigner负性方法，还为设计和执行非高斯性检测实验提供了可行的协议，并可进一步发展为定量度量。

Abstract: Gaussian states are fundamental in continuous-variable quantum information, yet characterizing non-Gaussianity remains challenging due to the non-convexity of the Gaussian set. Existing witnesses typically rely on Wigner negativity or other information-theoretic quantities. In this work, we develop a group-theoretic, multi-copy approach to detect non-Gaussianity in bosonic systems. We study passive linear optical transformations that mix copies of a quantum state and analyze their commutation with identical Gaussian unitaries applied to each copy. Orthogonal copy-mixing transformations commute with the symplectic part of the Gaussian action, while the displacement part restricts the symmetry to the stabilizer of the collective mode. This structure yields a family of witnesses satisfied by all single-mode Gaussian states. Fixing the thermal reference parameter via the purity, violation of these identities certifies non-Gaussianity. We illustrate the method with several single-mode examples and present an experimental protocol based on passive interferometry and photon-number-resolved detection, showing that the relevant multi-copy expectation values can be estimated from bounded phase observables. Finally, we extend the construction to multi-mode systems and discuss how the same symmetry framework may lead to quantitative measures of non-Gaussianity.

</details>


### [96] [Optimal and Deterministic Quantum Search on the Simplex of Complete Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03777)
*Kiyoji Huang Fujiwara,Yujia Shi,Thomas G. Wong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次对完全图单纯复形（即一阶截断单纯格）上的量子搜索给出了解析证明，证明当桥权重等于M时算法达到最优O(√N)运行时间，并数值证明权重≥√M时也能达到最优运行时间，且在此区间内算法是确定性的（成功概率为1）。这是该图上首个同时具备渐近最优和确定性的量子搜索结果。对于非确定性情况，作者还给出了通过检查邻居顶点来找到标记顶点的方法，并首次表明在该图族内部，连通性也不是量子搜索速度的可靠指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在完全图单纯复形（由M+1个完全图组成，每个含M个顶点，任意两个子图之间由桥连接）上进行连续时间量子游走搜索单个标记顶点。此前的结果要么数值上证明最优O(√N)运行时间，要么解析地证明确定性成功概率为1，但从未同时实现两者，即使在桥加权的情况下也是如此。该工作旨在填补这一空白，给出既渐近最优又确定性的解析证明。

Method: 采用连续时间量子游走（CTQW）在加权完全图单纯复形上进行搜索。作者解析分析了当桥权重等于M时的情况，证明了最优性；同时通过数值方法展示了当权重≥√M时运行时间达到最优。对于非确定性权重区间，提出了通过检查邻居顶点来定位标记顶点的策略。

Result: 1) 首次解析证明当桥权重等于M时，量子搜索在完全图单纯复形上达到最优O(√N)运行时间；2) 数值证明当权重≥√M时即可达到最优运行时间；3) 当权重在√M和M之间时算法是确定性的（成功概率为1）；4) 对于非确定性情况，给出通过检查邻居顶点来找到标记顶点的方法；5) 证明连通性在该图族内部也不是量子搜索速度的可靠指标。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了完全图单纯复形上量子搜索的解析最优性证明与确定性成功概率的统一，填补了此前数值与解析结果之间的空白。同时拓展了对量子搜索中图拓扑（特别是连通性）与算法性能关系的理解，表明即便在同一图族内部，连通性也不是衡量量子搜索效率的可靠指标。

Abstract: The simplex of complete graphs, also known as the first-order truncated simplex lattice, is a network of $M+1$ identical complete graphs, each with $M$ vertices, such that each clique contains an edge or bridge to every other clique. It contains $N = M(M+1)$ vertices, and previous asymptotic results using a continuous-time quantum walk to search this graph for a single marked vertex have either numerically demonstrated an optimal runtime of $O(\sqrt{N})$, or analytically proved a deterministic success probability of 1, but not both, even when the bridges are weighted. In this paper, we give the first analytical proof of optimal quantum search on this graph, proving that it occurs when the weight of the bridges equals $M$. In addition, we numerically show that the optimal runtime is achieved more broadly whenever the weight is at least $\sqrt{M}$. Furthermore, the algorithm is also deterministic when the weight scales between $\sqrt{M}$ and $M$, and this is the first example of quantum search on the simplex of complete graphs that is both asymptotically optimal and deterministic. In addition, for weights where the algorithm is nondeterministic, we give a way to find the marked vertex by inspecting neighboring vertices. Finally, while it is known that connectivity is not a reliable indicator of fast quantum search when comparing different graph families, we show that it is also unreliable within the graph family of weighted simplex of complete graphs.

</details>


### [97] [Impossibility of Perfectly Complete Many-Round Key Agreement in the QROM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03824)
*Longcheng Li,Qian Li,Xingjian Li,Qipeng Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在量子安全的单向函数（OWFs）的黑盒假设下，无法构造完美完备的量子密钥协商协议（QKA）。具体地，对于任意只交换经典消息、分别对布尔值随机预言机进行qA和qB次量子查询的Alice-Bob协议，存在窃听者仅需O((qA+qB)^5)次经典预言机查询即可确定性地恢复共享密钥，且该界限与轮数、传输长度、密钥长度和预言机域大小无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于量子密钥协商协议不可构造性的结果仅适用于两轮密钥协商协议（Li等，CRYPTO 26），或依赖于未经证明的猜想（Austrin等，CRYPTO 22）。本文旨在给出一个不依赖任何未证猜想且适用于任意轮数的通用不可能性证明。

Method: 作者考虑Alice和Bob仅交换经典消息、并分别对布尔值随机预言机进行量子查询的协议模型。通过构造性的攻击策略，证明存在一个窃听者，在仅观察到经典消息的情况下，可以使用O((qA+qB)^5)次经典预言机查询确定性恢复共享密钥。该证明采用了黑盒归约的技术路线，将密钥协商的安全性归约到量子单向函数的不可破解性上，从而得出矛盾。

Result: 证明了一个强不可能性结果：量子安全单向函数无法在黑盒方式下构造完美完备的量子密钥协商协议。攻击者的查询复杂度上界为O((qA+qB)^5)，且该上界与协议轮数、传输长度、密钥长度以及预言机域大小均无关。这是首个不依赖未证猜想、适用于任意轮数的此类结果。

Conclusion: 完美完备的量子密钥协商协议无法仅从量子安全单向函数通过黑盒构造获得，即需要更强的密码学假设或非黑盒技术来实现此类协议。此外，论文提到该证明由GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra在一次对话中发现，作者对论文的正确性、撰写和讨论负全责。

Abstract: This paper proves that it is impossible to construct perfectly complete quantum key agreement protocols (QKA) from quantumly secure one-way functions (OWFs) in a black-box manner.
  Specifically, consider any protocol in which Alice and Bob exchange only classical messages, make at most $q_{\mathsf{A}}$ and $q_{\mathsf{B}}$ quantum queries, respectively, to a Boolean-valued random oracle, and agree on a shared key with certainty. This paper shows that there exists an eavesdropper, given the classical messages, that can recover the shared key with certainty using $O((q_{\mathsf{A}}+q_{\mathsf{B}})^5)$ classical oracle queries. The bound is independent of the number of rounds, transcript length, key length, and oracle-domain size. Previous results only applies to two-round key agreement (Li et al. CRYPTO 26) or relies on unproven conjectures (Austrin et al. CRYPTO 22).
  GPT-5.6 Sol Ultra found this proof in a one-shot conversation and drafted a preliminary version of this paper. The authors are fully responsible for the correctness, writing and discussions of this paper.

</details>


### [98] [CNOT-Distance is NP-complete under all-to-all connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03825)
*Antonio Acuaviva,Arturo Acuaviva,Pablo Acuaviva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了判定一个GL(N,2)中的矩阵是否能用至多K个CNOT门（全连接固定标记线）实现的问题是NP完全的。通过从有限简单图构造上三角幺模矩阵，建立了CNOT电路复杂度与最小顶点覆盖问题之间的联系，并进一步证明了近似问题的APX困难性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路综合中，CNOT门是最基本的受控逻辑门之一，最小化CNOT门数量对量子电路优化至关重要。此前缺乏对该优化问题的计算复杂性严格界定，本文旨在证明CNOT电路综合问题在最坏情况下的计算难度。

Method: 从有限简单图G构造上三角幺模矩阵A_G，满足CNOT复杂度等于2|V|+2|E|+τ(G)。下界通过将任意CNOT电路展开为XOR有向无环图并应用投影-收缩操作，允许取消和任意重用中间奇偶项。通过多项式时间解码器将CNOT电路优化归约到三次图上的最小顶点覆盖问题（L-归约），从而证明APX困难性。

Result: 证明CNOT门数最小化问题是NP完全的；该问题族的最优解不受任意有限数量的干净或借用的辅助线（需恢复）影响；通过L-归约从三次图最小顶点覆盖得到APX困难性，并证明任意固定加性常数内的近似是NP困难的。

Conclusion: CNOT电路综合的最小化问题是计算上困难的（NP完全且APX困难），即使在矩阵具有O(N)个非零元素且行Hamming权重至多4的稀疏情况下依然成立，这为量子电路优化算法的设计提供了复杂性下界依据。

Abstract: Given $A\in\operatorname{GL}(N,2)$ and an integer $K$, we ask whether $A$ can be implemented by at most $K$ CNOT gates on fixed labelled wires with all-to-all connectivity. We prove that this problem is NP-complete. From a finite simple graph $G=(V,E)$, we construct an upper-unitriangular matrix $A_G\in\operatorname{GL}(2|V|+|E|+1,2)$ satisfying $\ell_{\mathrm{CNOT}}(A_G)=2|V|+2|E|+τ(G)$, where $τ(G)$ is the minimum vertex-cover size. Each target matrix has $O(N)$ nonzero entries and row Hamming weight at most four. The lower bound unfolds an arbitrary CNOT circuit into an XOR directed acyclic graph and applies projection--contraction operations, allowing cancellation and unrestricted reuse of intermediate parities. For this family, the optimum is unchanged by any finite number of clean or borrowed ancillary wires that must be restored. A polynomial-time decoder further yields NP-hardness of approximation within every fixed additive constant and, through an L-reduction from Minimum Vertex Cover on cubic graphs, APX-hardness of the associated CNOT-circuit optimisation problem.

</details>


### [99] [Separable States Violate the Complementary-Quantum Correlation Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03828)
*Jinbo Wang,Qihang Wang,Kun Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文驳斥了互补量子关联猜想（complementary-quantum correlation conjecture），该猜想断言互补基下测量的两个经典互信息之和不会超过测量前的量子互信息。作者在所有局部维度d≥3上给出了显式的秩二可分态反例，其中两个互补测量分别恢复完整的一位分支标签和额外的经典关联。对于qutrit，超出量恰好为1/3·log2(3456/3125)=0.0484156759比特。通过连续性可推广到满秩可分态，且该效应既不需要纠缠也不需要量子失协，而是源于对同一经典隐变量的两次互补读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究互补基测量所获取的经典互信息之和与测量前量子互信息之间的关系，验证互补量子关联猜想是否成立。该猜想若成立将是量子信息论中一个优雅的边界，但作者通过构造反例来检验其普适性。

Method: 在任意局部维度d≥3下显式构造了一个秩二的可分量子态，并证明该态在两个互补基测量下的经典互信息之和严格超过其量子互信息。利用qutrit（d=3）情况给出了精确的超额数值，并通过连续性论证扩展到满秩可分态。

Result: 在所有局部维度d≥3上成功构造了反例，推翻互补量子关联猜想。qutrit情况下超额精确为1/3·log2(3456/3125)≈0.0484比特。该效应在可分态上即可实现，既不涉及纠缠也不涉及量子失协。

Conclusion: 互补量子关联猜想被证伪。有趣的是，这种超出量来自对同一经典隐变量的两种互补读出方式，说明经典信息本身在互补基测量下也能产生超出量子互信息的关联总和。该结果对量子关联测量的理解具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Correlations measured in complementary local bases provide an experimentally accessible probe of the total correlations in a bipartite quantum state. The complementary-quantum correlation conjecture asserts that the sum of two such classical mutual informations never exceeds the premeasurement quantum mutual information. We disprove this conjecture in every local dimension $d\geq3$ with an explicit rank-two separable state. One of the two complementary measurements recovers the complete one-bit branch label, while the other retains additional classical correlation. For qutrits the excess is exactly $\frac13\log_2(3456/3125)=0.0484156759\ldots$ bits. Continuity yields full-rank separable violations. The effect therefore requires neither entanglement nor quantum discord; it arises from two complementary readouts of the same classical latent variable.

</details>


### [100] [Geometric-Symmetry Logical Gate and Local-Probe Selectivity in the Three-Leg AKLT Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03861)
*Jingnuo Han,Youning Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了三腿AKLT梯子作为一种具有SO(3)×Z₂ onsite对称性的精确可解SPT系统，利用矩阵乘积态构造刻画了边界编码空间的对称性作用及局域算子的可达性，并通过Knill-Laflamme条件启发的可区分性度量推导出对称性分辨的局域可达性衰减定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPT相为在受保护的边界自由度中编码量子信息提供了平台，但系统地理解何种对称性保护边界信息以及局域算子在何种程度下可以访问这些边界编码空间仍然缺乏定量刻画。作者希望通过精确可解模型建立SPT对称性、晶格几何与边界量子信息保护之间的定量联系。

Method: 采用三腿AKLT梯子作为精确可解模型，利用精确矩阵乘积态（MPS）构造来表征边界编码空间；分析SO(3)连续对称性引起的边界旋转和腿交换对称性产生的几何相关的逻辑置换；引入基于Knill-Laflamme条件启发的可区分性度量，结合Wigner-Eckart定理推导出对称性分辨的衰减定律。

Result: 发现连续SO(3)对称性诱导边界旋转，腿交换对称性产生几何依赖的逻辑比特置换；推导出局域算子可达性的衰减定律，其衰减由Wigner-Eckart定理产生的选择定则控制（rank-ℓ局域算子仅耦合到L=ℓ传递矩阵扇区），衰减长度由相应关联长度决定；还识别出一个与探针算子位置无关的有限尺寸通道。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了SPT对称性、晶格几何与边界编码量子信息保护之间的定量联系，揭示了对称性如何决定边界量子信息的可访问性和保护程度。

Abstract: Symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases provide a platform for encoding quantum information in protected boundary degrees of freedom. Here we study the three-leg Affleck-Kennedy-Lieb-Tasaki (AKLT) ladder as an exactly solvable SPT system with on-site symmetry $SO(3)\times \mathbb{Z}_2$. Using an exact matrix product state construction, we characterize the symmetry action on the edge encoding space and the accessibility of this space by local operators. We find that the continuous $SO(3)$ symmetry induces boundary rotations, while the leg-exchange symmetry generates a geometry-dependent logical permutation of edge qubits. Furthermore, by introducing a distinguishability measure motivated by the Knill--Laflamme condition, we derive a symmetry-resolved decay law for local accessibility. The decay is controlled by a selection rule raised from the Wigner--Eckart theorem, whereby a rank-$\ell$ local operator couples only to the $\mathcal L=\ell$ transfer-matrix sector, with a decay length determined by the corresponding correlation length. We further identify a finite-size channel that is independent of the probe operator position. These results establish a quantitative connection between SPT symmetry, lattice geometry, and the protection of boundary-encoded quantum information.

</details>


### [101] [Interplay between teleportation fidelity and basis-independent coherence in maximally sliced states under decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03865)
*Anushree Pandey,Sovik Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了环境退相干对量子隐形传态的影响，以三比特最大切片（MS）态作为共享纠缠资源，通过Kraus算子形式推导了振幅阻尼和相位阻尼通道下的隐形传态保真度解析表达式，建立了相干性与保真度之间的显式解析关系，并利用Coffman-Kundu-Wootters三卷缠（CKW三体纠缠度）表述结果，揭示了两种噪声通道对量子隐形传态性能的差异化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子隐形传态在实际环境中不可避免受到环境退相干的影响，而现有研究缺乏对三比特MS态在噪声通道下隐形传态性能的系统性解析分析，尤其是纠缠、相干性与传态保真度之间的内在关联尚未得到统一刻画。

Method: 采用Kraus算子形式主义，对三比特MS态分别构建振幅阻尼和相位阻尼两种退相干通道的数学模型，解析推导传态保真度表达式；进一步计算基无关（basis-independent）相干性，建立相干性与保真度间的解析关系，并将结果用CKW三卷缠量表示，实现三体纠缠、相干性和隐形传态性能的统一联系。

Result: 两种噪声通道产生截然不同的效应：振幅阻尼引入依赖初始状态的隐形传态量子优势阈值，当噪声超过该阈值时量子隐形传态失效；而相位阻尼在完全退相位之前始终保持量子优势。同时获得了相干性与传态保真度的显式解析关系，并通过CKW三卷缠将三体纠缠与传态性能直接关联。

Conclusion: 该工作为噪声环境中三比特MS态的量子隐形传态研究提供了统一的分析框架，系统阐明了多体纠缠、量子相干性与隐形传态性能之间的相互作用机制，为理解实际量子通信中噪声的影响提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The influence of environmental decoherence on quantum teleportation is investigated by considering the three-qubit Maximally Sliced (MS) state as the shared entangled resource. Using the Kraus operator formalism, analytical expressions are derived for the teleportation fidelity under amplitude damping and phase damping channels. The corresponding basis-independent coherence is obtained, establishing explicit analytical relations between coherence and teleportation fidelity under both decoherence mechanisms. The results are further expressed in terms of the Coffman-Kundu-Wootters (CKW) three-tangle, thereby connecting genuine tripartite entanglement with teleportation performance. The analysis reveals distinct effects of the two noise channels: amplitude damping introduces a state-dependent threshold for achieving quantum teleportation, whereas phase damping preserves the quantum advantage until complete dephasing. These results provide a unified analytical framework for understanding the interplay among multipartite entanglement, quantum coherence and teleportation in noisy three-qubit MS states.

</details>


### [102] [High-level quantum structured programs as quantum registers compositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03873)
*David Chamizo,Jose Garcia-Alonso,Juan M. Murillo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子编程的高层抽象框架，将量子计算视为由量子寄存器组成的结构，通过代数形式化定义语义清晰的变换操作（如条件相移和并行求值），并基于此设计了量子SMT求解器，旨在降低量子程序设计的认知负担和错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子程序主要基于量子门级抽象的单个量子比特操作，在大规模和复杂问题上给程序员带来高认知负担，导致程序规范繁琐且易出错。需要提供更高的抽象机制来提高编程效率和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出概念框架，将量子计算视为由量子寄存器组成的结构（每个寄存器是不可分割的实体）；通过代数形式化建立高层语法与低层语义之间的桥梁，语法基于量子算法中常用的操作（逻辑条件满足时的相移和并行求值）；基于这些形式化操作设计量子SMT求解器。

Result: 提出了一个基于代数形式化的高层量子编程语法框架，并在此基础上设计了一个量子SMT求解器，验证了该框架的可行性与实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作为实现高层量子结构化编程建立了一些方法论原则，对推动量子编程从门级向更高抽象层次的演进具有贡献意义。

Abstract: Current quantum programs are mainly designed at the level of quantum gates acting on individual qubits; on a large scale and for complex problems this may involve a high cognitive load on the programmer, making the program specification nontrivial and error-prone. In this context, providing quantum programming with higher abstraction mechanisms will assist in making this task more manageable and robust against design errors. In this work, a conceptual framework is addressed following the notion of the whole quantum computation as a structure composed of quantum registers representing each an undivided entity. Thus, computation progresses through semantically well-defined transformations that act on, or entangle, quantum registers, thereby modifying the global state. Ultimately, the program reaches the desired state by following a specific composition strategy. With this in mind, high-level syntax is presented through an algebraic formalism that bridges them with their low-level semantics. Proposed syntax is based on certain well-know operations used on quantum algorithms that apply phase shifts upon logical condition satisfaction or leverage on parallel evaluation. Based solely on the formalized operations, a quantum satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) solver can be designed. At its core, this work contributes to establishing some methodological principles towards realizing a high-level quantum structured programming.

</details>


### [103] [Exact Tradeoff Between Quantum Error Correction and Quantum Darwinism: An Information-Theoretic No-Go Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03944)
*Arghya Maity,Kelvin Onggadinata,Teck Seng Koh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了量子纠错（QEC）与量子达尔文主义之间的定量联系。通过基于Shor [[9,1,3]]码的逻辑GHZ块集体耦合N个环境量子比特的可精确求解模型，作者系统计算了逻辑保真度、Holevo信息与达尔文冗余度，揭示了冗余经典记录的出现以牺牲逻辑量子信息为代价。此外，他们还证明了一个模型无关的no-go定理：逻辑保真度超过临界阈值将阻碍达尔文冗余的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错与量子达尔文主义分别描述了系统-环境相互作用的对立后果——前者旨在保护逻辑量子信息，后者解释客观经典信息如何通过环境增殖而涌现。尽管二者物理根源相同，但此前缺乏直接的定量关联。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，建立两个范式间的信息论桥梁。

Method: 构建基于Shor [[9,1,3]]量子纠错码的逻辑GHZ块模型，将其集体耦合至N个环境量子比特，形成可精确求解的块环境模型。对任意环境尺寸和非完美恢复效率，系统计算逻辑保真度、Holevo信息和达尔文冗余度。通过消去公共退相干参数，得到将达尔文冗余度直接与恢复后逻辑保真度关联的精确权衡关系。此外，从一般信息论原理出发证明了一个模型无关的no-go定理。

Result: 获得了精确的权衡关系，证明冗余经典记录的出现以牺牲逻辑量子信息为代价；推导出模型无关的no-go定理，表明逻辑保真度超过临界阈值即排除达尔文冗余出现的可能性；该可解模型恰好饱和此一般界限。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子逻辑信息保护与冗余经典记录涌现之间首个定量信息论联系，为理解量子纠错与量子达尔文主义两种看似对立的范式的内在统一提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) and Quantum Darwinism describe opposing consequences of system-environment interactions: QEC seeks to preserve logical quantum information, whereas Quantum Darwinism explains the emergence of objective classical information through its proliferation into the environment. Despite their common physical origin, no direct quantitative connection between these paradigms has previously been established. We introduce an exactly solvable block-environment model based on the logical GHZ block of the Shor [[9,1,3]] quantum error-correcting code, collectively coupled to N environment qubits. The logical fidelity, Holevo information, and Darwinistic redundancy are obtained systematically for arbitrary environment size and imperfect recovery efficiency. Eliminating the common decoherence parameter yields an exact tradeoff relating Darwinistic redundancy directly to the post-recovery logical fidelity, demonstrating that the emergence of redundant classical records occurs at the expense of logical quantum information. We further prove a model-independent no-go theorem showing that the logical fidelity exceeds a critical threshold precludes the emergence of Darwinistic redundancy, irrespective of the microscopic Hamiltonian or environment structure. The solvable model saturates this general bound, establishing the first quantitative information-theoretic connection between logical quantum information protection and the emergence of redundant classical records.

</details>


### [104] [Real-time decoding of quantum error correction codes using high-performance computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03948)
*Lingling Lao,Qiang Wang,Yuanqi Liu,Yantong Liu,Haowen Wang,Yitao Chen,Yankang Zhao,Zhenwei Wu,Wei Zhang,Yong Dong,Yingwen Liu,Mingche Lai,Junjie Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出THQLink架构，基于TH-Express网络连接HPC与QPU控制系统，实现量子纠错码的实时解码。平均往返延迟2.944μs，每跳增量开销130ns，采用并行窗口策略可在CPU上实现距离19表面码的实时解码（每轮1μs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错需要严格的实时解码，即解码器必须在微秒量级内处理症候测量并确定纠错动作以避免数据积压。随着逻辑量子比特数量的扩展，对计算资源的需求显著增加，亟需高性能计算资源来支撑实时解码。

Method: 提出THQLink架构，利用高性能计算（HPC）资源进行实时解码。网络基于TH-Express构建，连接HPC与量子处理器（QPU）控制系统，可适配不同量子技术及其控制栈。采用并行窗口策略，在CPU上实现基于匹配的解码器。

Result: 平均往返延迟为2.944μs，每增加一跳的增量开销为130ns。通过并行窗口策略，在CPU上实现了距离19的表面码实时解码，每轮QEC耗时1μs。

Conclusion: 该工作为容错量子计算中的实时解码提供了可扩展框架，可应用于量子中心超级计算机（QPU与HPC紧密集成），高效支持混合量子-经典算法及从QPU卸载的计算密集型工作负载。

Abstract: Quantum error correction (QEC) is indispensable for building scalable fault-tolerant quantum computers. Effective QEC demands stringent real-time decoding: the decoder must process syndrome measurements and determine corrections within a time scale--typically on the order of microseconds, to avoid data backlog. Scaling to large number of logical qubits further necessitates significant computational resources. In this work, we propose an architecture, called \emph{THQLink}, for real-time decoding of quantum error correction codes using high-performance computing (HPC) resources. The network connecting the HPC and the control system of quantum processing unit (QPU) is built on TH-Express and can be adapted to different quantum technologies and their associated control stacks. We report a round-trip latency of 2.944 $μ$s on average, with an incremental overhead of 130 ns per additional hop. Using a parallel window strategy, we demonstrate real-time decoding (1 $μ$s per QEC round) of the surface code up to distance 19 using a matching-based decoder on CPUs. Our work presents a scalable framework for real-time decoding in fault-tolerant quantum computing. It can be readily applied to quantum-centric supercomputers that feature tight integration between QPU and HPC resources, thereby enabling efficient support for hybrid quantum-classical algorithms and computation-intensive workloads offloaded from the QPU.

</details>


### [105] [Separating quantum circuits from classical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03962)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Arkopal Dutt,Hari Krovi,Rik Sengupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了低深度量子计算与有界资源经典语言模型架构（包括扩散语言模型和Transformer）之间在预测和生成两大任务上的无条件分离，分别给出分布级分离（QNC⁰电路可采样、而常数轮DLM无法在常数距离内采样）和函数级分离（land∘QNC⁰[log log n]电路可计算、而常数深度Transformer需n^{Ω(1)}宽度），开创了大规模语言模型时代量子优势的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大规模语言模型（包括Transformer和扩散语言模型）分别围绕预测（next-token prediction）和生成（denoising）两大核心任务构建。作者希望探究：在这样的语言模型架构中，是否存在仅靠有界（浅层）资源无法完成、而低深度量子电路可以完成的任务？这既回应了量子计算优势在经典深度学习时代是否延续的问题，也首次将量子复杂性理论引入大语言模型架构的分析。

Method: 作者采用复杂性理论中的电路下界方法：(1) 在分布级分离中，构造一个可由QNC⁰（常数深度有界扇入量子电路）采样的特定概率分布，并证明任何常数轮扩散语言模型（DLM），即使允许亚线性chain-of-thought和输出token修正/remasking，也无法在常数统计距离内采样该分布；(2) 在函数级分离中，构造一个可由land∘QNC⁰[log log n]电路（O(log log n)深度QNC⁰后接一个经典AND门）计算的布尔函数，并证明任何常数深度、仅含decoder-only的Transformer计算该函数所需宽度至少为n^{Ω(1)}。

Result: 两项无条件分隔结果均成立：分布级上，QNC⁰可采样的分布在常数轮浅层级DLM下不可采样（即使引入chain-of-thought与remasking等现代特性）；函数级上，land∘QNC⁰[log log n]可计算的函数要求常数深度decoder-only Transformer具有多项式宽度（n^{Ω(1)}），即浅层经典架构在该问题上必然"大而宽"。

Conclusion: 本文建立了低深度量子计算与关键经典语言模型架构之间的无条件计算分离，表明即使考虑现代DLM的链式思考与token修订能力以及Transformer的嵌套注意力机制，经典浅层架构在特定预测/生成任务上仍存在本质局限，而QNC⁰电路可突破这些界限。这为语言模型时代量子计算优势的系统性研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern large language models - transformers and diffusion language models - are built around two canonical algorithmic tasks: prediction and generation. We prove unconditional separations between low-depth quantum computation and the corresponding bounded-resource classical language-model architectures in both regimes. Concretely, we exhibit the following:
  1. Distributional separation. We give a distribution that is sampleable by $\textsf{QNC}^0$ circuits (i.e., a family of constant-depth quantum circuits consisting of bounded fan-in gates) that no constant-round diffusion language model ($\textsf{DLM}$) with shallow scheduling and denoising can sample within constant distance, even when allowed sublinear chain-of-thought and output-token revision/remasking events, the very features modern $\textsf{DLM}$s rely on.
  2. Functional separation. We exhibit a function computable in $\land \circ \textsf{QNC}^0[\log\log n]$ (i.e., a family of O$(\log\log n)$-depth $\textsf{QNC}^0$ circuits, where $n$ is the input length, followed by a single classical $\mathsf{AND}$ gate) such that any constant-depth decoder-only transformer computing the function must be large: it would have to have width $n^{Ω(1)}$.
  Together, our work initiates the study of quantum advantage in the era of large language models.

</details>


### [106] [A quantum game of telephone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03963)
*Arefur Rahman,Matthew L. Stevens,Cory M. Nunn,Daniel E. Jones,Brian T. Kirby,Joseph M. Lukens*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"量子电话"协议，一种迭代式辅助辅助过程层析成像方法，利用中间探测事件将已探测链路作为后续链路的辅助量子比特，在部署的多节点光纤网络上实现了无需全域局部纠缠源的多节点量子网络表征，并通过下游近幺正通道的边界约束解决了顺序估计中的参数简并问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多节点量子网络的表征需要全域的局部纠缠源，这在实验上极具挑战性；标准的辅助辅助过程层析成像要求严格满秩条件，且噪声中间通道引起的参数简并会在顺序估计中累积推理误差。

Method: 提出"量子电话"协议：一种迭代的辅助辅助过程层析成像方案，利用中间探测事件将先前探测的链路作为后续链路的辅助量子比特；在部署的多节点光纤网络上实现，并结合全局推理利用下游近幺正通道提供的边界约束来消除上游通道的歧义，同时补偿局部信息损失。

Result: 在多节点光纤网络上观察到噪声中间通道造成的基本参数简并会加剧顺序估计下的推理误差；但发现下游近幺正通道的引入提供了边界约束，与全局推理配合可解决序列中较早通道的歧义；该方法即使中间态完全去极化也能无需严格满秩条件。

Conclusion: 量子电话协议提供了一条硬件高效且信息最大化的路径，用于在资源受限条件下表征复杂量子网络。

Abstract: Characterizing multinode quantum networks without ubiquitous local entanglement sources presents significant experimental challenges. We introduce ``quantum telephone,'' an iterative ancilla-assisted process tomography protocol that leverages intermediate detection events, effectively treating previously probed links as ancillae for subsequent links. Implemented on a deployed multinode fiber network, we observe noisy intermediate channels create fundamental parameter degeneracies that compound inference errors under sequential estimation. Counterintuitively, the inclusion of downstream near-unitary channels provides boundary constraints that, when used in tandem with global inference, can resolve ambiguity in channels earlier in the sequence. By compensating for localized information loss, this approach obviates the strict full-rank requirements of standard ancilla-assisted process tomography, even when intermediate states become completely depolarized. Overall, quantum telephone offers a hardware-efficient and information-maximizing path toward characterizing complex quantum networks with limited resources.

</details>


### [107] [A lower bound on the classical simulation cost of star-network correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03986)
*Martin J. Renner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了星型网络中联合测量的量子通信复杂性，引入了一种排他性任务（exclusion task），该任务在各方发送量子d维系统时可完美解决，但若发送经典消息则需每方至少n^(d-1)个符号才能确定性地解决。这展示了量子消息相对经典消息的通信优势，且该优势同时随量子系统维度和同时测量的系统数量扩展。作为应用，该结果表明任何有限大小的经典描述都无法重现足够多量子比特联合测量的统计特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子策略在多种通信任务中优于经典策略已被充分证实，但需要进一步研究星型网络分布场景下联合测量产生的关联的量子通信复杂性，特别是量化量子消息相对经典消息的优势。

Method: 论文引入了一种排他性任务（exclusion task），该任务要求各方将量子系统发送到中央节点进行联合测量。通过分析该任务的通信复杂度下界，证明经典消息需要至少n^(d-1)个符号才能确定性解决，而量子d维系统可以完美解决。

Result: 该排他性任务在各方发送量子d维系统时可完美解决，但在n方各发送少于n^(d-1)个符号的经典消息时无法确定性解决，从而证明了量子消息在通信中的优势，该优势同时随量子系统维度d和系统数量n扩展。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，不存在有限大小的经典描述能够完全重现足够多个量子比特上联合测量的统计特性，这为量子通信优势提供了新的理论和应用证据。

Abstract: It is well established that quantum strategies outperform classical ones in several communication tasks. We study the quantum communication complexity of correlations arising from joint measurements on quantum systems distributed across a star network, where several parties each send a quantum system to a central node. We introduce an exclusion task that can be solved perfectly when each party sends a quantum $d$-level system, but would require a large classical message otherwise. In fact, the task cannot be solved with certainty if each of the $n$ parties sends a classical message with less than $n^{(d-1)}$ symbols. This implies an advantage of using quantum over classical messages in that scenario that scales with both, the dimension of the quantum system and the number of systems measured simultaneously. As an application, this shows that no finite-size classical description of a qubit suffices to reproduce the statistics of a joint measurement on sufficiently many qubits.

</details>


### [108] [Realified tensor networks: quantum circuit simulation on real-valued matrix accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03987)
*Yusheng Zhao,Xiwei Pan,Enji Xiong,Chengkai Zhu,Jinguo Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"实化重写"(realification)方法，将任意复数张量网络映射为实数张量网络，使其能在仅支持实数GEMM的现代矩阵加速器（NPU/TPU）上原生高效运行。该方法通过Gauss三乘法(3M)公式构造秩3结构张量，实现了不超过3×的算术开销上界，中间张量大小最多翻倍。在67个量子电路上验证了开销定律的准确性，并在Ascend 910 NPU上显著优于四实数GEMM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路模拟的核心是张量网络收缩，但现代矩阵加速器（NPU、TPU）仅暴露实数GEMM流水线，复数张量网络必须在软件层重建，导致效率不匹配。现有的四实数GEMM方法开销高，缺乏从复数网络到实数网络的结构化映射方案。

Method: 提出"实化重写"(realification)变换：将任意复数张量网络映射为实数张量网络。关键在于每次合并两个复数张量时，构造一个秩3的结构张量来实现Gauss三乘法(3M)公式；单复数操作数的收缩仅需2个或1个实数乘积。作者证明了一个紧致的开销定律：实数乘法开销为1+2m+r，其中m和r分别代表双复数与单复数收缩的体积分数，总体不超过实数收缩的3×，且中间张量大小最多翻倍。通过与低温度模拟退火结合的收缩顺序迁移策略，实现从复数网络到实数网络的算术开销最小化。

Result: 在67个电路（随机电路、Clifford+T、QAOA、VQE）上验证了开销定律在实数到复数范围内的准确性；影响成本的是复数门的位置而非数量。66/67个电路上收缩顺序迁移的相对算术开销差距低于5×10⁻⁴，唯一例外经少量模拟退火步骤即可收敛。在Ascend 910 NPU上，该重写方法在全部12个随机电路和52/55个结构化单元上击败了四实数GEMM基线和逐GEMM的Gauss降阶方法；四实数GEMM基线在随机电路上中位慢1.7×，在结构化电路上中位慢1.4×。

Conclusion: 实化重写使复数张量网络收缩能够在仅支持实数的矩阵引擎上原生运行，提供了严格的算术开销上界（不超过实数收缩的3×）并实证了其有效性，为量子电路模拟在AI加速硬件上的高效部署开辟了道路。

Abstract: Tensor-network contraction simulates quantum circuits, but modern matrix accelerators (NPUs, TPUs) expose only real GEMM pipelines, so the complex networks of quantum simulation must be reconstructed in software. We resolve the mismatch by a realification rewrite that maps any complex tensor network to a real one. At each merge of two complex tensors, a rank-3 structure tensor realizes Gauss's three-multiplication (3M) formula; contractions with one or no complex operand need only two or one real products. We prove a tight cost law: overhead $1 + 2m + r$ in real multiplications, where $m$ and $r$ are the volume fractions of two- and one-complex-operand contractions, never exceeding $3\times$ relative to real contraction, with every intermediate at most doubled in size. On 67 circuits (random, Clifford+$T$, QAOA, VQE), the law holds across the real-to-complex range and complex-gate placement, not count, governs cost. Contraction orders transfer from the complex network with a relative arithmetic-cost gap below $5\times 10^{-4}$ on 66 of 67 circuits; the exception closes under a few steps of low-temperature simulated annealing. On an Ascend 910 NPU the rewrite beat both the four-real-GEMM baseline and a per-GEMM Gauss lowering on all twelve random circuits and on 52 of 55 structured cells (three cells slower by at most 12\%); the four-GEMM baseline was slower by a median $1.7\times$ (random) and $1.4\times$ (structured). Realification makes complex tensor-network contraction native to real-only matrix engines.

</details>


### [109] [Non-Relativistic Quantum Electrodynamics of Atoms in a Rotating Ring Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03997)
*Jarrod T. Reilly,Murray J. Holland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从第一性原理出发，在非惯性旋转环形腔中推导量子光学模型。从弯曲时空中的Dirac方程出发，加入电磁场最小耦合，通过Foldy-Wouthuysen变换取非相对论极限，投影到原子费米Fock空间，再经Power-Zienau-Woolley变换从最小耦合规范转到多极展开规范，最终得到包含旋转效应（旋转诱导超精细频移和Sagnac频移）的Jaynes-Cummings-like哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子光学模型大多在惯性系中构建，而旋转（非惯性）系统中的原子-腔相互作用机制尚不清楚。作者希望从第一性原理出发，严格推导旋转环形腔中的量子光学哈密顿量，揭示旋转对原子跃迁和腔模的具体影响，特别是那些可能在小旋转速率下仍可观测的效应。

Method: 从弯曲时空中的Dirac方程出发，对广义Born度规加入电磁场最小耦合，得到Dirac哈密顿量；通过Foldy-Wouthuysen变换形式化地取非相对论极限，投影到原子电子、质子、中子的费米Fock空间；针对protium原子，在偶极和长波长近似下实施Power-Zienau-Woolley变换，从最小耦合规范转换到多极展开规范；最后在电偶极、两能级和旋波近似下得到Jaynes-Cummings-like哈密顿量。

Result: 推导出包含旋转效应的Jaynes-Cummings-like哈密顿量，发现了两个关键旋转效应：一是旋转诱导的超精细频移（即使在小旋转速率下也可能被实验观测），二是腔中反向传播模式的Sagnac频移。

Conclusion: 从第一性原理严格构建了旋转非惯性环形腔中的原子-光相互作用量子光学模型，证明了旋转不仅通过Sagnac效应影响腔模频率，还会通过超精细频移影响原子跃迁，为后续实验验证和更精确的旋转敏感量子器件设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we derive a quantum optics model in a non-inertial rotating ring cavity from first principles. We begin with the Dirac equation in curved spacetime, add minimal coupling to the electromagnetic field, and then find the Dirac Hamiltonian for the generalized Born metric. We then formally take the non-relativistic limit by way of Foldy-Wouthuysen transformations and project onto a fermionic Fock space for the atoms' electrons, protons, and neutrons. Focusing on a protium atom, we next move from a minimal coupling gauge to a multipole expansion gauge by taking a Power-Zienau-Woolley transformation under the dipole and long-wavelength approximations. Here, we find additional terms from the rotation of the system including a rotation-induced hyperfine shift of the atomic transition which could possibly be observed experimentally even for small rotation rates. Making the electric dipole, two-level, and rotating-wave approximations, we arrive at a Jaynes-Cummings-like Hamiltonian which also accounts for rotational effects, such as the rotation-induced hyperfine shift and the Sagnac shift for the cavity's counterpropagating modes.

</details>

### [110] [Faraday pattern formation in dipolar superfluid and supersolid quantum gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03553)
*Sumit Semwal,Shawan K. Jha,G. A. Bougas,S. I. Mistakidis,Pankaj Kumar Mishra*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维参数驱动下偶极量子气体中的法拉第不稳定性，发现超流体区共振激发离散多边形表面子谐波模式，超固相区则出现表面-体混合激发乃至体模式的交叉转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索长程相互作用（偶极相互作用）量子流体在参数驱动下的模式激发与图案形成机制，理解从超流到超固相转变过程中法拉第不稳定性的演化规律。

Method: 基于扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程进行三维数值模拟，通过周期性调制短程相互作用产生参数驱动，并用Mathieu方程理论解析验证共振频率与不稳定参数窗口。

Result: 发现离散多边形表面模式的亚谐波共振，Mathieu方程精确捕获共振频率与不稳定窗口；偶极相互作用使共振频率相较非偶极对应系统向低频移动；在超流-超固相转变附近，软化roton模式加速图案形成并产生混合表面-体激发，而超固相深处体模式由液滴与超流背景碰撞产生。

Conclusion: 证实了从表面集体模式到混合表面-体激发的交叉，展示了参数驱动如何主导长程相互作用量子流体的图案形成。

Abstract: We investigate Faraday instabilities in three-dimensional parametrically driven trapped dipolar quantum gases within the framework of the extended Gross-Pitaevskii equation. In the superfluid regime, periodic modulation of the short-range interactions induces the resonant excitation of discrete polygonal surface modes displaying sub-harmonic response. The resonance frequencies and the parametric windows of instability are accurately captured by an appropriate Mathieu equation, further corroborating our simulations. It is also shown that dipolar interactions in superfluids shift the resonance frequencies to lower values compared to their non-dipolar counterparts. Near the superfluid-to-supersolid transition, intrinsic density undulations associated with the softened roton mode accelerate pattern formation, yielding hybrid surface and bulk excitations. The bulk patterns prevail deeper in the supersolid regime, emerging from droplet collisions with the superfluid background. Our results reveal a crossover from surface collective modes to hybrid surface-bulk excitations and demonstrate how parametric driving dictates pattern formation in long-range interacting quantum fluids.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Impurities as Probes of Finite-Temperature Fluctuations in Two-Dimensional Bose Gases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03665)
*Victor Velasco,Gabriele Spada,Giovanni Midei,Andrea Perali,Luis A. Peña Ardila*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究了有限尺寸弱相互作用二维玻色气体中的吸引杂质（玻色极化子），结合有限温度多体散射理论和路径积分蒙特卡洛方法，发现红外动量尺度带来的声子激活温度使杂质能量先近似温度无关，随后在声子热布居与凝聚体耗散竞争下呈现非单调温度依赖，表明吸引玻色极化子可作为低维玻色气体中热涨落、声子修飾和束缚态物理的灵敏探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维量子气体中热涨落、有限尺寸效应和二体束缚态形成相互交织，研究吸引杂质在这种环境中的准粒子行为，揭示有限尺寸如何稳定凝聚体并影响极化子能量，从而更好地理解低维量子多体系统中的杂质物理。

Method: 采用混合方法：将有限温度多体散射理论与路径积分蒙特卡洛（PIMC）输入相结合。有限尺寸引入红外动量截断稳定非零凝聚分数，使得玻戈留波夫（Bogoliubov）描述有效，进而分析杂质准粒子在凝聚态和正常态区域的能量。

Result: 红外动量尺度产生一个声子激活温度：低于该温度时杂质能量几乎与温度无关；当声子模式被热布居后，声子贡献与凝聚体耗散相竞争，导致极化子能量随温度呈非单调变化。

Conclusion: 吸引玻色极化子有望作为有限尺寸系统中热涨落、声子修饰和束缚态物理的灵敏探针，为低维玻色气体的量子杂质研究提供了新的理论见解。

Abstract: Two-dimensional quantum gases provide a distinctive setting in which enhanced thermal fluctuations, finite-size effects, and two-body bound-state formation are intrinsically intertwined. In this work, we study a single attractive impurity immersed in a finite, weakly interacting two-dimensional Bose gas, where finite size stabilizes a nonzero condensate fraction by introducing an infrared momentum scale, thereby enabling a Bogoliubov description of the bath. Using a hybrid approach that combines finite-temperature many-body scattering theory with input from path-integral Monte Carlo, we analyze the impurity quasiparticle energy across the condensate and normal regimes. The infrared scale generates a phonon-activation temperature below which the impurity energy remains nearly temperature independent. Once the resolved phonon modes become thermally populated, their contribution competes with condensate depletion, producing a nonmonotonic temperature dependence of the polaron energy. These results suggest that attractive Bose polarons may serve as sensitive probes of finite-size thermal fluctuations, phonon dressing, and bound-state physics in low-dimensional Bose gases.

</details>
